# GasGas EX 250 2023: aftermarket parts

4498 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 282 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 1651 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 2565 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
